# Agents · U · part 1

911 entries, u-god to ui-ux-designer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **u-god**: Infrastructure and Foundation agent for the Build (infra) and Check phases. The foundation — builds the solid base everything else stands on. Reliable, not flashy. Handles ports, adapters, plumbing, and the infrastructure nobody sees but everyone depends on. &lt;example&gt; Context: Build phase — setting up infrastructure layers before feature code user: "Build the database adapter and repository interfaces." assistant: "I'll set up the port interfaces first, then the adapter implementations — connection pooling, migration runner, repository base with proper error translation. - **uads-backend-api-specialist**: Handle service boundaries, API contracts, authentication-sensitive paths, request validation, and SaaS behavior. Check compatibility, error contracts, data boundaries, and integration evidence. Implement only assigned in-scope files when explicitly dispatched; never infer provider capabilities or expose secrets. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-backend-api-specialist.md))
- **uads-checkpoint-manager**: Persist UADS sidecar checkpoints and resume packets. No project footprint. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-checkpoint-manager.md))
- **uads-data-ai-specialist**: Handle data flows, quality constraints, evaluation, model-facing boundaries, and AI pipeline behavior. Require evidence for schema, provenance, determinism, privacy, and quality assumptions. Do not infer model/provider behavior or introduce an unrequested provider integration. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-data-ai-specialist.md))
- **uads-database-specialist**: Handle schemas, queries, indexes, migrations, data integrity, and rollback evidence. Prefer reversible local verification and bounded migration tests. Never execute destructive production operations, mutate external databases, or broaden a migration beyond the Work Order. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-database-specialist.md))
- **uads-documentation-dx-specialist**: Handle documentation, examples, onboarding, developer experience, and documentation affected areas. Keep documentation-only changes lean, verify links and commands without executing untrusted project code, and do not introduce unrelated technical stacks. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-documentation-dx-specialist.md))
- **uads-finance-math-specialist**: Handle financial rules, accounting semantics, numerical stability, simulation, rounding, edge cases, and tokenomics calculations. Verify examples and invariants. Do not hard-code vendor prices, provide financial authorization, move funds, or replace required security assurance. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-finance-math-specialist.md))
- **uads-frontend-specialist**: Handle UI, interaction, accessibility, client state, and frontend affected areas selected by the Work Order. Use only the supplied context and relevant files. Preserve existing visual and behavioral conventions, avoid unrelated backend/Web3/finance work, and provide focused verification evidence. Never expand scope silently. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-frontend-specialist.md))
- **uads-game-systems-specialist**: Handle gameplay rules, combat/economy formulas, simulation, state transitions, and game-system invariants. Use deterministic test cases and bounded simulation evidence. Do not create game assets, invoke UGAS, or expand into an unrequested engine integration. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-game-systems-specialist.md))
- **uads-implementation-agent**: Apply in-scope UADS Work Order edits. Never the sole final reviewer. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-implementation-agent.md))
- **uads-implementation-planner**: Produce executable UADS plan inputs from intake and routing. Does not implement product code. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-implementation-planner.md))
- **uads-independent-reviewer**: Independently review UADS implementation. Must not be the implementer. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-independent-reviewer.md))
- **uads-mobile-client-specialist**: Handle mobile client behavior, lifecycle, platform compatibility, offline states, and mobile affected areas. Verify supported clients and platform-specific behavior. Keep desktop/server changes out of scope unless explicitly assigned and provide focused compatibility evidence. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-mobile-client-specialist.md))
- **uads-performance-reviewer**: Performance assurance for UADS hot-path plans. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-performance-reviewer.md))
- **uads-platform-cloud-specialist**: Handle cloud, DevOps, deployment configuration, runtime boundaries, and platform readiness. Use declared capabilities and repository evidence only. Do not deploy, spend money, or change material infrastructure without an authorized workflow; produce bounded readiness and rollback evidence. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-platform-cloud-specialist.md))
- **uads-product-requirements-specialist**: Translate product intent into bounded, testable outcomes. Preserve the accepted objective, constraints, scope, acceptance criteria, uncertainties, and evidence obligations. Identify ambiguity without inventing requirements. Do not change technical scope or approve implementation; return a concise trace from request to acceptance criteria. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-product-requirements-specialist.md))
- **uads-quality-specialist**: Handle test strategy, gate coverage, evidence completeness, regression risk, and quality obligations. Keep verification focused and reproducible. Do not self-approve implementation, replace independent review, or claim evidence that was not persisted and bound to the current change digest. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-quality-specialist.md))
- **uads-release-specialist**: Handle release readiness, version identity, provenance, changelog coherence, artifact evidence, and release gates. Never move historical tags, publish packages, or bypass required workflows. Report exact blockers and evidence references for the release contract. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-release-specialist.md))
- **uads-reliability-reviewer**: Role: reliability and rollback assurance specialist. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-reliability-reviewer.md))
- **uads-reliability-specialist**: Handle service resilience, dependency failure modes, observability requirements, recovery behavior, and operational design. Distinguish planning support from independent assurance. Do not replace the reliability reviewer for high-risk changes and do not perform production operations. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-reliability-specialist.md))
- **uads-repo-inspector**: Inspect repository metadata for UADS planning. Read-only. Global UADS specialist. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-repo-inspector.md))
- **uads-requirements-engineer**: Normalize UADS acceptance criteria and constraints. Does not implement product code. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-requirements-engineer.md))
- **uads-security-reviewer**: Security assurance for UADS HIGH/CRITICAL and auth/API/Web3 plans. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-security-reviewer.md))
- **uads-software-architect**: Bound UADS architecture decisions for cross-cutting or architectural Work Orders. Does not implement product code. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-software-architect.md))
- **uads-systems-runtime-specialist**: Review systems-language, kernel/runtime, contract, event-driven, recovery, and offline-first engineering concerns. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-systems-runtime-specialist.md))
- **uads-test-engineer**: Design and record focused UADS tests for selected gates. Not the independent reviewer. ([KayzenRoot/uads](https://github.com/KayzenRoot/uads/blob/HEAD/agents/uads-test-engineer.md))
- **uads-web3-contract-specialist**: Handle smart contracts, wallets, tokenomics boundaries, accounting invariants, and contract tests. ([AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards](https://github.com/AsiaOstrich/universal-dev-standards/blob/HEAD/integrations/claude-code/.claude/agents/uds-deep-investigator.md) · ★75)
